Mathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology

CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.HasLeftHomologyclassMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, predicate
Wording
S has a left homology (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be a short complex of $C$. Then we can say that $S$ has a left homology.
Formula
$S \in \operatorname{ShortComplex} (C)\vdash \operatorname{HasLeftHomology} (S)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $S$ un nombre complexe court de $C$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $S$ a une homologie gauche.
CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.LeftHomologyDatastructureMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, type
Wording
left homology data of S (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be a short complex of $C$. Then the left homology data of $S$ are a basic type.
Formula
$S \in \operatorname{ShortComplex} (C)\vdash \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (S)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $S$ un nombre complexe court de $C$. Alors les données d'homologies gauches de $S$ sont un type de base.
CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.LeftHomologyData.HdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a left homology data of $S$. Then $\operatorname{H}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of $C$ defined as $\operatorname{proj1}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (S)\vdash \operatorname{H} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{proj1} (\mathrm{self})\in C$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ une donnée d'homologies gauche de $S$. Alors $\operatorname{H}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ est un élément de $C$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj1}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.

CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.LeftHomologyData.copydefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, function or constant
Wording
the copy of eK and eH (from the name)
English
Let $h$ be a left homology data of $S$. Let $\mathrm{eK}$ be an isomorphism of $\mathrm{K'}$ and $\operatorname{K}$ applied to $h$. Let $\mathrm{eH}$ be an isomorphism of $\mathrm{H'}$ and $\operatorname{H}$ applied to $h$. Then the copy of $\mathrm{eK}$ and $\mathrm{eH}$ is a left homology data of $S$.
Formula
$h \in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (S), \mathrm{eK} \in \operatorname{Iso} (\mathrm{K'} , \operatorname{K} (h)), \mathrm{eH} \in \operatorname{Iso} (\mathrm{H'} , \operatorname{H} (h))\vdash \operatorname{copy} (h , \mathrm{eK} , \mathrm{eH})\in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (S)$
French
Soit $h$ une donnée d'homologies gauche de $S$. Soit $\mathrm{eK}$ un isomorphisme de $\mathrm{K'}$ et $\operatorname{K}$ appliqué à $h$. Soit $\mathrm{eH}$ un isomorphisme de $\mathrm{H'}$ et $\operatorname{H}$ appliqué à $h$. Alors la copie de $\mathrm{eK}$ et $\mathrm{eH}$ est une donnée d'homologies gauche de $S$.
CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.LeftHomologyData.cyclesIsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, function or constant
Wording
the isomorphism of h (from the docstring)
English
Let $h$ be a left homology data of $S$. Then the isomorphism of $h$ is an isomorphism of the cycle of $S$ and $\operatorname{K}$ applied to $h$ defined as the cycle map isomorphism of the left homology data of $S$ and $h$.
Formula
$h \in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (S)\vdash \operatorname{cyclesIso} (h):= \operatorname{cyclesMapIso'} (\operatorname{refl} (S), \operatorname{leftHomologyData} (S), h)\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{cycles} (S), \operatorname{K} (h))$
French
Soit $h$ une donnée d'homologies gauche de $S$. Alors l'isomorphisme de $h$ est un isomorphisme du cycle de $S$ et $\operatorname{K}$ appliqué à $h$ défini comme l'isomorphisme d'applications de cycles de la donnée d'homologies gauche de $S$ et $h$.
CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.LeftHomologyData.descHdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, function or constant
Wording
the induced morphism of h and k (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $h$ be a left homology data of $S$. Let $k$ be a homomorphism of $\operatorname{K}$ applied to $h$ and $A$ such that the composition of $\operatorname{f'}$ applied to $h$ and $k$ is equal to $0$. Then the induced morphism of $h$ and $k$ is a homomorphism of $\operatorname{H}$ applied to $h$ and $A$ defined as the induced morphism of the projection of $h$ and the projection of $k$ and $\mathrm{hk}$.
Formula
$h \in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (S), k \in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{K} (h), A), \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{f'} (h), k)}{0}\vdash \operatorname{descH} (h , k , \mathrm{hk}):= \operatorname{desc} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ShortComplex\_\_LeftHomologyData\_\_h\_u3c0\_} (h), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Limits\_\_CokernelCofork\_\_of\_u3c0\_} (k , \mathrm{hk}))\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{H} (h), A)$
French
Soit $h$ une donnée d'homologies gauche de $S$. Soit $k$ un homomorphisme de $\operatorname{K}$ appliqué à $h$ et $A$ tel que la composition de $\operatorname{f'}$ appliqué à $h$ et $k$ soit égale à $0$. Alors le morphisme induit de $h$ et $k$ est un homomorphisme de $\operatorname{H}$ appliqué à $h$ et $A$ défini comme le morphisme induit de la projection de $h$ et la projection de $k$ et $\mathrm{hk}$.

Wording
the lift of h and k (from the name)
English
Let $h$ be a left homology data of $S$. Let $k$ be a homomorphism of $A$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ShortComplex\_\_X\_u2082\_}$ applied to $S$ such that the composition of $k$ and $\operatorname{g}$ applied to $S$ is equal to $0$. CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.LeftHomologyData.ofEpiOfIsIsoOfMonodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, function or constant
Wording
the left homology data of x4 and h (from the name)
English
Let $x_{4}$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $h$ be a left homology data of $x_{2}$. Then the left homology data of $x_{4}$ and $h$ is a left homology data of $x_{3}$.
Formula
$x_{4} \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{2} , x_{3}), h \in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (x_{2})\vdash \operatorname{ofEpiOfIsIsoOfMono} (x_{4} , h)\in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (x_{3})$
French
Soit $x_{4}$ un homomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Soit $h$ une donnée d'homologies gauche de $x_{2}$. Alors la donnée d'homologies gauche de $x_{4}$ et $h$ est une donnée d'homologies gauche de $x_{3}$.

CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.LeftHomologyData.ofIsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.LeftHomology, function or constant
Wording
the left homology data of e and x4 (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be an isomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $x_{4}$ be a left homology data of $x_{2}$. Then the left homology data of $e$ and $x_{4}$ is a left homology data of $x_{3}$ defined as the left homology data of the homomorphism of $e$ and $x_{4}$.
Formula
$e \in \operatorname{Iso} (x_{2} , x_{3}), x_{4} \in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (x_{2})\vdash \operatorname{ofIso} (e , x_{4}):= \operatorname{ofEpiOfIsIsoOfMono} (\operatorname{hom} (e), x_{4})\in \operatorname{LeftHomologyData} (x_{3})$
French
Soit $e$ un isomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Soit $x_{4}$ une donnée d'homologies gauche de $x_{2}$. Alors la donnée d'homologies gauche de $e$ et $x_{4}$ est une donnée d'homologies gauche de $x_{3}$ défini comme la donnée d'homologies gauche de l'homomorphisme de $e$ et $x_{4}$.

F preserves a homology (from the name)

Let $F$ be a functor of $C$ and $D$. Then the natural isomorphism of $F$ is an isomorphism of the composition of the map short complex of $F$ and the homology functor of $D$ and the composition of the homology functor of $C$ and $F$.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Functor} (C , D)\vdash \operatorname{homologyFunctorIso} (F)\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{mapShortComplex} (F), \operatorname{homologyFunctor} (D)), \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{homologyFunctor} (C), F))$
French
Soit $F$ un foncteur de $C$ et $D$. Alors l'isomorphisme naturel de $F$ est un isomorphisme de la composition du nombre complexe court d'applications de $F$ et le foncteur d'homologies de $D$ et la composition du foncteur d'homologies de $C$ et $F$.

CategoryTheory.Abelian.SpectralObjectstructureMathlib.Algebra.Homology.SpectralObject.Basic, type
Wording
spectral object of C and x (from the name)
English
Let $C$ and $x$ be sets. Then the spectral objects of $C$ and $x$ are a basic type.
Formula
$C , x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{SpectralObject} (C , x)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soient $C$ et $x$ des ensembles. Alors les objets spectraux de $C$ et $x$ sont un type de base.
CategoryTheory.Abelian.SpectralObject.HdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.SpectralObject.Basic, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a spectral object of $C$ and $x$. Let $n$ be an integer. Then $\operatorname{H}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $n$ is a functor of the set of composable arrows of $x$ and $1$ and $C$ defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $n$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{SpectralObject} (C , x), n \in \Integer \vdash \operatorname{H} (\mathrm{self} , n):= \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self} , n)\in \operatorname{Functor} (\operatorname{ComposableArrows} (x , 1), C)$
French

the cycle of g and n (from the name)
English
Let $X$ be a spectral object of $C$ and $x$. Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $i$ and $j$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $j$ and $k$. Let $n$ be an integer. Then the cycle of $g$ and $n$ is a homomorphism of the cycle of $g$ and $n$ and the action of $\operatorname{H}$ applied to $X$ and $n$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ComposableArrows\_\_mk\_u2081\_}$ applied to $g$.
Formula
$X \in \operatorname{SpectralObject} (C , x), f \in \operatorname{Hom} (i , j), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (j , k), n \in \Integer \vdash \operatorname{iCycles} (X , f , g , n)\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{cycles} (X , f , g , n), \operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{H} (X , n), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ComposableArrows\_\_mk\_u2081\_} (g)))$
French
Soit $X$ un objet spectral de $C$ et $x$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $i$ et $j$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $j$ et $k$. Soit $n$ un entier. Alors le cycle de $g$ et $n$ est un homomorphisme du cycle de $g$ et $n$ et l'action de $\operatorname{H}$ appliqué à $X$ et $n$ et $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ComposableArrows\_\_mk\_u2081\_}$ appliqué à $g$.

Wording

English

Let $x_{4}$ and $x_{5}$ be integers such that $\mathrm{autoParam}$ holds for $\mathrm{Eq} (x_{4} + 1 , x_{5})$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Abelian\_\_SpectralObject\_\_kernelSequenceCycles\_\_\_auto\_1}$. Formula

Wording

English
Let $X$ be a spectral object of $C$ and $x$. Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $i$ and $j$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $j$ and $k$. Let $\mathrm{fg}$ be a homomorphism of $i$ and $k$ such that the composition of $f$ and $g$ is equal to $\mathrm{fg}$. Let $n$ be an integer. Then $\operatorname{kernelSequenceOpcycles}$ applied to $X$, $f$, $g$, $\mathrm{fg}$, $h$ and $n$ is a short complex of $C$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{fromOpcycles}$ applied to $X$, $f$, $g$, $\mathrm{fg}$, $h$ and $n$, the map of $\operatorname{H}$ applied to $X$ and $n$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ComposableArrows\_\_two\_u3b4\_\_u2081\_To\_u3b4\_\_u2080\_}$ applied to $f$, $g$, $\mathrm{fg}$ and $h$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Abelian\_\_SpectralObject\_\_fromOpcycles\_H\_map\_two\_u3b4\_\_u2081\_To\_u3b4\_\_u2080\_}$ applied to $X$, $f$, $g$, $\mathrm{fg}$, $h$ and $n$.
Formula

French

CategoryTheory.Abelian.SpectralObject.liftCyclesdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.SpectralObject.Cycles, function or constant
Wording
the lift cycle of x5 and x6 (from the name)
English
Let $X$ be a spectral object of $C$ and $x$. Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $i$ and $j$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $j$ and $k$. Let $x_{4}$ and $x_{5}$ be integers such that $x_{4} + 1 = x_{5}$. Let $x_{6}$ be a homomorphism of $A$ and the action of $\operatorname{H}$ applied to $X$ and $x_{4}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ComposableArrows\_\_mk\_u2081\_}$ applied to $g$ such that the composition of $x_{6}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Abelian\_\_SpectralObject\_\_\_u3b4\_}$ applied to $X$, $f$, $g$, $x_{4}$, $x_{5}$ and $h$ is equal to $0$. Then the lift cycle of $x_{5}$ and $x_{6}$ is a homomorphism of $A$ and the cycle of $g$ and $x_{4}$.
Formula
$X \in \operatorname{SpectralObject} (C , x), f \in \operatorname{Hom} (i , j), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (j , k), x_{4} , x_{5} \in \Integer , x_{4} + 1 = x_{5} , x_{6} \in \operatorname{Hom} (A , \operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{H} (X , x_{4}), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ComposableArrows\_\_mk\_u2081\_} (g))), \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{comp} (x_{6} , \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Abelian\_\_SpectralObject\_\_\_u3b4\_} (X , f , g , x_{4} , x_{5} , h))}{0}\vdash \operatorname{liftCycles} (X , f , g , x_{4} , x_{5} , h , x_{6} , \mathrm{hx})\in \operatorname{Hom} (A , \operatorname{cycles} (X , f , g , x_{4}))$
French
Soit $X$ un objet spectral de $C$ et $x$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $i$ et $j$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $j$ et $k$. Soient $x_{4}$ et $x_{5}$ des entiers tels que $x_{4} + 1 = x_{5}$. Soit $x_{6}$ un homomorphisme de $A$ et l'action de $\operatorname{H}$ appliqué à $X$ et $x_{4}$ et $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ComposableArrows\_\_mk\_u2081\_}$ appliqué à $g$ tel que la composition de $x_{6}$ et $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Abelian\_\_SpectralObject\_\_\_u3b4\_}$ appliqué à $X$, $f$, $g$, $x_{4}$, $x_{5}$ et $h$ soit égale à $0$. Alors le cycle de relèvements de $x_{5}$ et $x_{6}$ est un homomorphisme de $A$ et le cycle de $g$ et $x_{4}$.
